CODE｜src/normals.cpp
======================================================================
來源：src/normals.cpp
SHA256：3b3841400fed1bbffe013a0e758da9a9c655bd6db8e41f47452c529d0763d8e4
行號僅供閱讀定位；不得當成可直接編譯內容。
0001 | #include "cm/normals.hpp"
0002 |
0003 | #include <pcl/octree/octree_search.h>
0004 |
0005 | #include <Eigen/Eigenvalues>
0006 | #include <algorithm>
0007 | #include <cmath>
0008 | #include <limits>
0009 |
0010 | namespace cm {
0011 |
0012 | NormalResult computeNormals(const Cloud& cloud, const NormalParams& p) {
0013 |   NormalResult r;
0014 |   const std::size_t n = cloud.size();
0015 |   r.normals.reset(new NormalCloud);
0016 |   r.normals->resize(n);
0017 |   r.verticality.assign(n, std::numeric_limits<float>::quiet_NaN());
0018 |   r.valid.assign(n, 0);
0019 |   if (n == 0) return r;
0020 |
0021 |   Cloud::Ptr c(new Cloud(cloud));
0022 |   pcl::octree::OctreePointCloudSearch<PointT> octree(p.octree_res);
0023 |   octree.setInputCloud(c);
0024 |   octree.addPointsFromInputCloud();
0025 |
0026 |   const float nan = std::numeric_limits<float>::quiet_NaN();
0027 | #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 256)
0028 |   for (long long i = 0; i < static_cast<long long>(n); ++i) {
0029 |     pcl::Indices nb;
0030 |     std::vector<float> d2;
0031 |     octree.radiusSearch((*c)[i], p.radius, nb, d2);
0032 |     NormalT& out = (*r.normals)[i];
0033 |     if (static_cast<int>(nb.size()) < p.min_neighbors) {
0034 |       out.normal_x = out.normal_y = out.normal_z = out.curvature = nan;
0035 |       continue;
0036 |     }
0037 |     // Octree result order depends on insertion order; sort by coordinates so the
0038 |     // floating-point sums (and thus verticality at the threshold) are order-independent.
0039 |     std::sort(nb.begin(), nb.end(), [&](int a, int b) {
0040 |       const auto &pa = (*c)[a], &pb = (*c)[b];
0041 |       if (pa.x != pb.x) return pa.x < pb.x;
0042 |       if (pa.y != pb.y) return pa.y < pb.y;
0043 |       return pa.z < pb.z;
0044 |     });
0045 |     Eigen::Vector3d C = Eigen::Vector3d::Zero();
0046 |     for (auto k : nb) C += (*c)[k].getVector3fMap().cast<double>();
0047 |     C /= double(nb.size());
0048 |     Eigen::Matrix3d cov = Eigen::Matrix3d::Zero();
0049 |     for (auto k : nb) {
0050 |       const Eigen::Vector3d dvec = (*c)[k].getVector3fMap().cast<double>() - C;
0051 |       cov += dvec * dvec.transpose();
0052 |     }
0053 |     cov /= double(nb.size());
0054 |     Eigen::SelfAdjointEigenSolver<Eigen::Matrix3d> es(cov);
0055 |     const Eigen::Vector3d e3 = es.eigenvectors().col(0);  // ascending eigenvalues
0056 |     const Eigen::Vector3d ev = es.eigenvalues();
0057 |     out.normal_x = float(e3.x());
0058 |     out.normal_y = float(e3.y());
0059 |     out.normal_z = float(e3.z());
0060 |     const double tr = ev.sum();
0061 |     out.curvature = tr > 0 ? float(ev(0) / tr) : 0.f;
0062 |     r.verticality[i] = float(1.0 - std::abs(e3.z()));
0063 |     r.valid[i] = 1;
0064 |   }
0065 |   for (char v : r.valid) r.n_invalid += v ? 0 : 1;
0066 |   return r;
0067 | }
0068 |
0069 | }  // namespace cm

來源：src/retention.cpp
SHA256：baeccc15e39b985958476032c2f066dba3fd9b870d47f830c4bdc44ecfc00db0
行號僅供閱讀定位；不得當成可直接編譯內容。
0001 | #include "cm/retention.hpp"
0002 |
0003 | #include <algorithm>
0004 | #include <cmath>
0005 | #include <sstream>
0006 | #include <stdexcept>
0007 |
0008 | namespace cm {
0009 |
0010 | RetentionKind parseRetentionKind(const std::string& s) {
0011 |   if (s == "step") return RetentionKind::Step;
0012 |   if (s == "linear_a") return RetentionKind::LinearA;
0013 |   if (s == "linear_mid") return RetentionKind::LinearMid;
0014 |   if (s == "physical") return RetentionKind::Physical;
0015 |   if (s == "none") return RetentionKind::None;
0016 |   throw std::runtime_error("unknown retention version '" + s + "' (step | linear_a | linear_mid | physical | none)");
0017 | }
0018 |
0019 | std::string toString(RetentionKind k) {
0020 |   switch (k) {
0021 |     case RetentionKind::Step: return "step";
0022 |     case RetentionKind::LinearA: return "linear_a";
0023 |     case RetentionKind::LinearMid: return "linear_mid";
0024 |     case RetentionKind::Physical: return "physical";
0025 |     case RetentionKind::None: return "none";
0026 |   }
0027 |   return "?";
0028 | }
0029 |
0030 | double RetentionModel::operator()(double d) const {
0031 |   double p = 0;
0032 |   switch (kind) {
0033 |     case RetentionKind::Step:
0034 |       p = d < 5.0 ? 0.5 : (d < 10.0 ? 0.75 : 1.0);
0035 |       break;
0036 |     case RetentionKind::LinearA:
0037 |     case RetentionKind::LinearMid:
0038 |       p = intercept + slope * d;
0039 |       break;
0040 |     case RetentionKind::Physical: {
0041 |       const double r = d / d0;
0042 |       p = std::max(p_min, r * r);
0043 |       break;
0044 |     }
0045 |     case RetentionKind::None:
0046 |       p = 1.0;
0047 |       break;
0048 |   }
0049 |   return std::clamp(p, 0.0, 1.0);
0050 | }
0051 |
0052 | RetentionModel RetentionModel::make(RetentionKind k, const RetentionParams& p) {
0053 |   RetentionModel m;
0054 |   m.kind = k;
0055 |   m.slope = p.linear_slope;
0056 |   m.d0 = p.physical_d0;
0057 |   m.p_min = p.physical_pmin;
0058 |   m.intercept = k == RetentionKind::LinearMid ? 0.375 : 0.5;
0059 |   m.label = toString(k);
0060 |   return m;
0061 | }
0062 |
0063 | std::string RetentionModel::describe() const {
0064 |   std::ostringstream o;
0065 |   o.precision(6);
0066 |   switch (kind) {
0067 |     case RetentionKind::Step: o << "step: d<5:0.5, 5<=d<10:0.75, d>=10:1"; break;
0068 |     case RetentionKind::LinearA:
0069 |     case RetentionKind::LinearMid: o << label << ": clamp(" << intercept << " + " << slope << " d, 0, 1)"; break;
0070 |     case RetentionKind::Physical: o << label << ": clip((d/" << d0 << ")^2, " << p_min << ", 1)"; break;
0071 |     case RetentionKind::None: o << "none: 1 (no distance-dependent down-sampling)"; break;
0072 |   }
0073 |   return o.str();
0074 | }
0075 |
0076 | std::uint64_t splitmix64(std::uint64_t x) {
0077 |   x += 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15ULL;
0078 |   x = (x ^ (x >> 30)) * 0xBF58476D1CE4E5B9ULL;
0079 |   x = (x ^ (x >> 27)) * 0x94D049BB133111EBULL;
0080 |   return x ^ (x >> 31);
0081 | }
0082 |
0083 | double commonUniform(std::uint64_t seed, const PointT& p, double quantum) {
0084 |   auto q = [&](float v) { return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::llround(double(v) / quantum)); };
0085 |   std::uint64_t h = splitmix64(seed);
0086 |   h = splitmix64(h ^ q(p.x));
0087 |   h = splitmix64(h ^ q(p.y));
0088 |   h = splitmix64(h ^ q(p.z));
0089 |   return static_cast<double>(h >> 11) * 0x1.0p-53;
0090 | }
0091 |
0092 | std::vector<RetentionBin> retentionBins(const std::vector<double>& d, const std::vector<char>& kept,
0093 |                                         const RetentionModel& m, double bin) {
0094 |   std::vector<RetentionBin> bins;
0095 |   double dmax = 0;
0096 |   for (double x : d) dmax = std::max(dmax, x);
0097 |   const int nb = static_cast<int>(std::floor(dmax / bin)) + 1;
0098 |   bins.resize(nb);
0099 |   for (int b = 0; b < nb; ++b) {
0100 |     bins[b].d_lo = b * bin;
0101 |     bins[b].d_hi = (b + 1) * bin;
0102 |   }
0103 |   for (std::size_t i = 0; i < d.size(); ++i) {
0104 |     auto& b = bins[static_cast<int>(std::floor(d[i] / bin))];
0105 |     ++b.n;
0106 |     b.kept += kept[i] ? 1 : 0;
0107 |     b.p_mean += m(d[i]);
0108 |   }
0109 |   for (auto& b : bins)
0110 |     if (b.n) b.p_mean /= double(b.n);
0111 |   return bins;
0112 | }
0113 |
0114 | }  // namespace cm

0001 | #include "cm/dtm.hpp"
0002 |
0003 | #include <Eigen/Dense>
0004 | #include <algorithm>
0005 | #include <cmath>
0006 | #include <limits>
0007 | #include <stdexcept>
0008 | #include <tuple>
0009 |
0010 | namespace cm {
0011 |
0012 | namespace {
0013 |
0014 | struct Sample {
0015 |   double x, y, z;
0016 |   bool valid = false;
0017 | };
0018 |
0019 | // Least-squares plane z = a + b(x-cx) + c(y-cy); false if < 3 points or degenerate.
0020 | bool fitPlane(const std::vector<const Sample*>& s, double cx, double cy, double* a, double* b, double* c) {
0021 |   if (s.size() < 3) return false;
0022 |   Eigen::MatrixXd A(s.size(), 3);
0023 |   Eigen::VectorXd z(s.size());
0024 |   for (std::size_t k = 0; k < s.size(); ++k) {
0025 |     A(k, 0) = 1.0;
0026 |     A(k, 1) = s[k]->x - cx;
0027 |     A(k, 2) = s[k]->y - cy;
0028 |     z(k) = s[k]->z;
0029 |   }
0030 |   Eigen::ColPivHouseholderQR<Eigen::MatrixXd> qr(A);
0031 |   qr.setThreshold(1e-6);
0032 |   if (qr.rank() < 3) return false;
0033 |   const Eigen::Vector3d sol = qr.solve(z);
0034 |   *a = sol(0);
0035 |   *b = sol(1);
0036 |   *c = sol(2);
0037 |   return std::isfinite(*a) && std::isfinite(*b) && std::isfinite(*c);
0038 | }
0039 |
0040 | }  // namespace
0041 |
0042 | Dtm Dtm::build(const Cloud& cloud, const DtmParams& p) {
0043 |   if (cloud.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("DTM: empty cloud");
0044 |   if (!(p.cell > 0)) throw std::runtime_error("DTM: cell must be > 0");
0045 |   Dtm d;
0046 |   d.cell_ = p.cell;
0047 |   double minx = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity(), miny = minx, maxx = -minx, maxy = -minx;
0048 |   for (const auto& q : cloud) {
0049 |     minx = std::min(minx, double(q.x)); maxx = std::max(maxx, double(q.x));
0050 |     miny = std::min(miny, double(q.y)); maxy = std::max(maxy, double(q.y));
0051 |   }
0052 |   d.x0_ = std::floor(minx / p.cell) * p.cell;
0053 |   d.y0_ = std::floor(miny / p.cell) * p.cell;
0054 |   d.nx_ = static_cast<int>(std::floor((maxx - d.x0_) / p.cell)) + 1;
0055 |   d.ny_ = static_cast<int>(std::floor((maxy - d.y0_) / p.cell)) + 1;
0056 |   if (static_cast<double>(d.nx_) * d.ny_ > 4e8) throw std::runtime_error("DTM: grid too large; increase dtm.cell");
0057 |   const int nx = d.nx_, ny = d.ny_;
0058 |   d.stats_.nx = nx;
0059 |   d.stats_.ny = ny;
0060 |
0061 |   // 1. bucket points by cell (sorted, deterministic), pick k-th percentile.
0062 |   std::vector<std::pair<std::int64_t, int>> keyed(cloud.size());
0063 |   for (std::size_t i = 0; i < cloud.size(); ++i) {
0064 |     const int ix = std::min(nx - 1, static_cast<int>(std::floor((cloud[i].x - d.x0_) / p.cell)));
0065 |     const int iy = std::min(ny - 1, static_cast<int>(std::floor((cloud[i].y - d.y0_) / p.cell)));
0066 |     keyed[i] = {static_cast<std::int64_t>(iy) * nx + ix, static_cast<int>(i)};
0067 |   }
0068 |   std::sort(keyed.begin(), keyed.end());
0069 |   std::vector<Sample> samples(static_cast<std::size_t>(nx) * ny);
0070 |   // (z, x, y, index): ties in z (common with quantised files) are broken by
0071 |   // coordinates, so the chosen sample does not depend on input order.
0072 |   std::vector<std::tuple<float, float, float, int>> zs;
0073 |   for (std::size_t b = 0; b < keyed.size();) {
0074 |     std::size_t e = b;
0075 |     while (e < keyed.size() && keyed[e].first == keyed[b].first) ++e;
0076 |     if (static_cast<int>(e - b) >= p.min_points) {
0077 |       zs.clear();
0078 |       for (std::size_t k = b; k < e; ++k) {
0079 |         const auto& q = cloud[keyed[k].second];
0080 |         zs.emplace_back(q.z, q.x, q.y, keyed[k].second);
0081 |       }
0082 |       std::size_t kth = static_cast<std::size_t>(std::floor(p.percentile / 100.0 * double(zs.size() - 1)));
0083 |       if (p.ground_select == "supported_lowest") {
0084 |         // Lowest point that has >= support_count points within support_dz above it:
0085 |         // drops isolated below-ground noise without depending on how many
0086 |         // non-ground (stem/canopy) points share the cell.
0087 |         std::sort(zs.begin(), zs.end());
0088 |         for (std::size_t k = 0; k < zs.size(); ++k) {
0089 |           std::size_t above = 0;
0090 |           for (std::size_t m = k + 1; m < zs.size() && std::get<0>(zs[m]) <= std::get<0>(zs[k]) + p.support_dz; ++m)
0091 |             ++above;
0092 |           if (int(above) >= p.support_count) { kth = k; break; }
0093 |         }
0094 |       } else if (p.ground_select != "percentile") {
0095 |         throw std::runtime_error("dtm.ground_select must be percentile or supported_lowest");
0096 |       } else {
0097 |         std::nth_element(zs.begin(), zs.begin() + kth, zs.end());
0098 |       }
0099 |       const auto& q = cloud[std::get<3>(zs[kth])];
0100 |       samples[keyed[b].first] = Sample{q.x, q.y, q.z, true};
0101 |       ++d.stats_.cells_with_sample;
0102 |     }
0103 |     b = e;
0104 |   }
0105 |
0106 |   auto gather = [&](int i, int j, int r, bool skip_self, std::vector<const Sample*>& out) {
0107 |     out.clear();
0108 |     for (int jj = std::max(0, j - r); jj <= std::min(ny - 1, j + r); ++jj)
0109 |       for (int ii = std::max(0, i - r); ii <= std::min(nx - 1, i + r); ++ii) {
0110 |         if (skip_self && ii == i && jj == j) continue;
0111 |         const Sample& s = samples[static_cast<std::size_t>(jj) * nx + ii];
0112 |         if (s.valid) out.push_back(&s);
0113 |       }
0114 |   };
0115 |
0116 |   // 1b. slope-based filter (Vosselman 2000): a sample higher than another sample
0117 |   // within slope_radius by more than max_slope * distance + slope_tol cannot be
0118 |   // ground (where ground is occluded, the lowest point of a cell is on a branch
0119 |   // or canopy, and so are its neighbours', so step 2 alone cannot reject it).
0120 |   // Compared against the original samples (single pass, order independent).
0121 |   std::vector<char> reject(samples.size(), 0);
0122 |   if (p.slope_filter) {
0123 |     const int rc = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(p.slope_radius / p.cell));
0124 |     for (int j = 0; j < ny; ++j)
0125 |       for (int i = 0; i < nx; ++i) {
0126 |         const Sample& s = samples[static_cast<std::size_t>(j) * nx + i];
0127 |         if (!s.valid) continue;
0128 |         bool bad = false;
0129 |         for (int jj = std::max(0, j - rc); jj <= std::min(ny - 1, j + rc) && !bad; ++jj)
0130 |           for (int ii = std::max(0, i - rc); ii <= std::min(nx - 1, i + rc) && !bad; ++ii) {
0131 |             const Sample& t = samples[static_cast<std::size_t>(jj) * nx + ii];
0132 |             if (!t.valid || (ii == i && jj == j)) continue;
0133 |             const double dist = std::hypot(s.x - t.x, s.y - t.y);
0134 |             if (dist <= p.slope_radius && s.z - t.z > p.max_slope * dist + p.slope_tol) bad = true;
0135 |           }
0136 |         if (bad) reject[static_cast<std::size_t>(j) * nx + i] = 1;
0137 |       }
0138 |     for (std::size_t k = 0; k < samples.size(); ++k)
0139 |       if (reject[k]) {
0140 |         samples[k].valid = false;
0141 |         ++d.stats_.samples_rejected_slope;
0142 |         reject[k] = 0;
0143 |       }
0144 |   }
0145 |
0146 |   // 2. reject samples far above their neighbours' plane (single pass on the remaining samples).
0147 |   std::vector<const Sample*> nb;
0148 |   for (int j = 0; j < ny; ++j)
0149 |     for (int i = 0; i < nx; ++i) {
0150 |       const Sample& s = samples[static_cast<std::size_t>(j) * nx + i];
0151 |       if (!s.valid) continue;
0152 |       gather(i, j, p.fit_radius, true, nb);
0153 |       double a, b, c;
0154 |       if (!fitPlane(nb, s.x, s.y, &a, &b, &c)) continue;
0155 |       if (s.z - a > p.outlier_above) reject[static_cast<std::size_t>(j) * nx + i] = 1;
0156 |     }
0157 |   for (std::size_t k = 0; k < samples.size(); ++k)
0158 |     if (reject[k]) {
0159 |       samples[k].valid = false;
0160 |       ++d.stats_.samples_rejected;
0161 |     }
0162 |
0163 |   // 3. local plane per cell.
0164 |   d.planes_.assign(samples.size(), Plane{});
0165 |   for (int j = 0; j < ny; ++j)
0166 |     for (int i = 0; i < nx; ++i) {
0167 |       const double cx = d.x0_ + (i + 0.5) * p.cell, cy = d.y0_ + (j + 0.5) * p.cell;
0168 |       Plane& pl = d.planes_[static_cast<std::size_t>(j) * nx + i];
0169 |       for (int r = p.fit_radius; r <= std::max(p.fit_radius, p.fill_max_radius); ++r) {
0170 |         gather(i, j, r, false, nb);
0171 |         if (fitPlane(nb, cx, cy, &pl.a, &pl.b, &pl.c)) {
0172 |           pl.valid = true;
0173 |           break;
0174 |         }
0175 |       }
0176 |       if (pl.valid) {
0177 |         ++d.stats_.cells_valid;
0178 |         if (!samples[static_cast<std::size_t>(j) * nx + i].valid) ++d.stats_.cells_filled;
0179 |       }
0180 |     }
0181 |   return d;
0182 | }
0183 |
0184 | double Dtm::eval(int i, int j, double x, double y) const {
0185 |   const Plane& pl = planes_[static_cast<std::size_t>(j) * nx_ + i];
0186 |   const double cx = x0_ + (i + 0.5) * cell_, cy = y0_ + (j + 0.5) * cell_;
0187 |   return pl.a + pl.b * (x - cx) + pl.c * (y - cy);
0188 | }
0189 |
0190 | std::optional<double> Dtm::height(double x, double y) const {
0191 |   const int ci = static_cast<int>(std::floor((x - x0_) / cell_));
0192 |   const int cj = static_cast<int>(std::floor((y - y0_) / cell_));
0193 |   if (ci < 0 || cj < 0 || ci >= nx_ || cj >= ny_) return std::nullopt;
0194 |   if (!planes_[static_cast<std::size_t>(cj) * nx_ + ci].valid) return std::nullopt;
0195 |   const double gx = (x - x0_) / cell_ - 0.5, gy = (y - y0_) / cell_ - 0.5;
0196 |   const int i0 = static_cast<int>(std::floor(gx)), j0 = static_cast<int>(std::floor(gy));
0197 |   const double fx = gx - i0, fy = gy - j0;
0198 |   double sum = 0, wsum = 0;
0199 |   for (int dj = 0; dj <= 1; ++dj)
0200 |     for (int di = 0; di <= 1; ++di) {
0201 |       const int i = i0 + di, j = j0 + dj;
0202 |       if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= nx_ || j >= ny_) continue;
0203 |       if (!planes_[static_cast<std::size_t>(j) * nx_ + i].valid) continue;
0204 |       const double w = (di ? fx : 1 - fx) * (dj ? fy : 1 - fy);
0205 |       sum += w * eval(i, j, x, y);
0206 |       wsum += w;
0207 |     }
0208 |   if (wsum <= 1e-12) return eval(ci, cj, x, y);
0209 |   return sum / wsum;
0210 | }
0211 |
0212 | std::vector<int> heightFilter(const Cloud& cloud, const Dtm& dtm, const HeightParams& p, std::size_t* no_dtm) {
0213 |   std::vector<int> out;
0214 |   std::size_t missing = 0;
0215 |   for (std::size_t i = 0; i < cloud.size(); ++i) {
0216 |     const auto h = dtm.height(cloud[i].x, cloud[i].y);
0217 |     if (!h) { ++missing; continue; }
0218 |     const double dz = cloud[i].z - *h;
0219 |     if (dz >= p.zmin - p.neg_tol && dz <= p.zmax) out.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
0220 |   }
0221 |   if (no_dtm) *no_dtm = missing;
0222 |   return out;
0223 | }

0225 | }  // namespace cm

0014 | class SeededCylinderModel : public pcl::SampleConsensusModelCylinder<PointT, NormalT> {
0015 |  public:
0016 |   using Base = pcl::SampleConsensusModelCylinder<PointT, NormalT>;
0017 |   using Ptr = std::shared_ptr<SeededCylinderModel>;
0018 |   explicit SeededCylinderModel(const Cloud::ConstPtr& c) : Base(c, /*random=*/false) {}
0019 |   // rng_gen_ holds a reference to rng_alg_, so this re-seeds all sampling.
0020 |   void reseed(std::uint32_t s) { this->rng_alg_.seed(s); }
0021 |   using Base::getSamples;
0022 | };
0023 |
0024 | struct Sub {
0025 |   Cloud::Ptr cloud{new Cloud};
0026 |   NormalCloud::Ptr normals{new NormalCloud};
0027 | };
0028 |
0029 | Sub extract(const Cloud& cloud, const NormalCloud& normals, const std::vector<int>& idx) {
0030 |   Sub s;
0031 |   s.cloud->reserve(idx.size());
0032 |   s.normals->reserve(idx.size());
0033 |   for (int i : idx) {
0034 |     s.cloud->push_back(cloud[i]);
0035 |     s.normals->push_back(normals[i]);
0036 |   }
0037 |   return s;
0038 | }
0039 |

0041 |
0042 | const char* toString(FitFail f) {
0043 |   switch (f) {
0044 |     case FitFail::None: return "ok";
0045 |     case FitFail::TooFewPoints: return "too_few_points";
0046 |     case FitFail::NoModel: return "no_model";
0047 |     case FitFail::LowInlierRatio: return "low_inlier_ratio";
0048 |     case FitFail::RadiusOutOfRange: return "radius_out_of_range";
0049 |     case FitFail::TiltTooLarge: return "tilt_too_large";
0050 |     case FitFail::NormalInconsistent: return "normal_inconsistent";
0051 |     case FitFail::ArcCoverageLow: return "arc_coverage_low";
0052 |     case FitFail::NoDtmIntersection: return "no_dtm_intersection";
0053 |     default: return "?";
0054 |   }
0055 | }
0056 |
0057 | std::uint32_t clusterSeed(std::uint64_t base, const Cloud& cloud, const std::vector<int>& idx) {
0058 |   double cx = 0, cy = 0, cz = 0;
0059 |   for (int i : idx) { cx += cloud[i].x; cy += cloud[i].y; cz += cloud[i].z; }
0060 |   const double n = idx.empty() ? 1.0 : double(idx.size());
0061 |   auto q = [](double v) { return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(std::llround(v / 1e-3)); };
0062 |   std::uint64_t h = splitmix64(base ^ 0x5EEDC71DULL);
0063 |   h = splitmix64(h ^ q(cx / n));
0064 |   h = splitmix64(h ^ q(cy / n));
0065 |   h = splitmix64(h ^ q(cz / n));
0066 |   return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(h >> 32);
0067 | }
0068 |
0069 | CylinderFit fitCylinder(const Cloud& cloud, const NormalCloud& normals, const std::vector<int>& idx,
0070 |                         const CylinderParams& p, std::uint32_t ransac_seed) {
0071 |   CylinderFit r;
0072 |   r.n_points = idx.size();
0073 |   r.ransac_seed = ransac_seed;
0074 |   for (int i : idx) r.centroid += cloud[i].getVector3fMap().cast<double>();
0075 |   if (!idx.empty()) r.centroid /= double(idx.size());
0076 |   if (static_cast<int>(idx.size()) < std::max(p.min_points, 3)) {
0077 |     r.fail = FitFail::TooFewPoints;
0078 |     r.fail_mask = 1u << int(FitFail::TooFewPoints);
0079 |     return r;
0080 |   }
0081 |   const Sub s = extract(cloud, normals, idx);
0082 |   SeededCylinderModel::Ptr model(new SeededCylinderModel(s.cloud));
0083 |   model->setInputNormals(s.normals);
0084 |   model->setNormalDistanceWeight(p.normal_weight);
0085 |   model->reseed(ransac_seed);
0086 |
0087 |   pcl::RandomSampleConsensus<PointT> sac(model, p.dist_threshold);
0088 |   sac.setMaxIterations(p.max_iterations);
0089 |   sac.setProbability(p.probability);
0090 |   sac.setNumberOfThreads(-1);  // no OpenMP inside RANSAC: keeps sampling order deterministic
0091 |   if (!sac.computeModel()) {
0092 |     r.fail = FitFail::NoModel;
0093 |     r.fail_mask = 1u << int(FitFail::NoModel);
0094 |     return r;
0095 |   }
0096 |   pcl::Indices inliers;
0097 |   sac.getInliers(inliers);
0098 |   Eigen::VectorXf coef;
0099 |   sac.getModelCoefficients(coef);
0100 |   if (coef.size() != 7) {
0101 |     r.fail = FitFail::NoModel;
0102 |     r.fail_mask = 1u << int(FitFail::NoModel);
0103 |     return r;
0104 |   }
0105 |   // LM refit (PCL's optimizeModelCoefficients: Euclidean point-to-surface
0106 |   // distance) on the GEOMETRIC inliers of the current model. Using PCL's
0107 |   // normal-weighted inliers here would drop points with poor normals and bias
0108 |   // the arc that constrains the radius. Repeated lm_passes times.
0109 |   auto geometricInliers = [&](const Eigen::VectorXf& c) {
0110 |     pcl::Indices out;
0111 |     Eigen::Vector3d ap(c[0], c[1], c[2]), d(c[3], c[4], c[5]);
0112 |     if (d.norm() < 1e-12) return out;
0113 |     d.normalize();
0114 |     for (std::size_t k = 0; k < s.cloud->size(); ++k) {
0115 |       const Eigen::Vector3d w = (*s.cloud)[k].getVector3fMap().cast<double>() - ap;
0116 |       if (std::abs((w - w.dot(d) * d).norm() - std::abs(double(c[6]))) <= p.dist_threshold) out.push_back(int(k));
0117 |     }
0118 |     return out;
0119 |   };
0120 |   if (p.lm_refit) {
0121 |     for (int pass = 0; pass < std::max(1, p.lm_passes); ++pass) {
0122 |       const pcl::Indices gi = geometricInliers(coef);
0123 |       if (gi.size() <= 7) break;
0124 |       Eigen::VectorXf refined;
0125 |       model->optimizeModelCoefficients(gi, coef, refined);
0126 |       if (refined.size() != 7 || !refined.allFinite()) break;
0127 |       coef = refined;
0128 |     }
0129 |     model->selectWithinDistance(coef, p.dist_threshold, inliers);
0130 |   }
0131 |   Eigen::Vector3d dir(coef[3], coef[4], coef[5]);
0132 |   if (dir.norm() < 1e-12) {
0133 |     r.fail = FitFail::NoModel;
0134 |     r.fail_mask = 1u << int(FitFail::NoModel);
0135 |     return r;
0136 |   }
0137 |   dir.normalize();
0138 |   if (dir.z() < 0) dir = -dir;
0139 |   r.axis_point = Eigen::Vector3d(coef[0], coef[1], coef[2]);
0140 |   r.axis_dir = dir;
0141 |   r.radius = std::abs(coef[6]);
0142 |   r.tilt_deg = std::acos(std::min(1.0, dir.z())) * 180.0 / M_PI;
0143 |   r.n_inliers_sac = inliers.size();
0144 |   const double cos_max = std::cos(p.normal_max_angle_deg * M_PI / 180.0);
0145 |   const Eigen::Vector3d u1 = dir.unitOrthogonal(), u2 = dir.cross(u1);
0146 |   constexpr int kArcBins = 72;  // 5 degrees
0147 |   std::vector<char> arc(kArcBins, 0);
0148 |   for (std::size_t k = 0; k < s.cloud->size(); ++k) {
0149 |     const Eigen::Vector3d w = (*s.cloud)[k].getVector3fMap().cast<double>() - r.axis_point;
0150 |     const Eigen::Vector3d radial = w - w.dot(dir) * dir;  // surface normal direction at q
0151 |     const double dist_axis = radial.norm();
0152 |     if (std::abs(dist_axis - r.radius) <= p.dist_threshold) {
0153 |       ++r.n_inliers;
0154 |       const double ang = std::atan2(radial.dot(u2), radial.dot(u1)) + M_PI;  // [0, 2pi]
0155 |       arc[std::min(kArcBins - 1, static_cast<int>(ang / (2 * M_PI) * kArcBins))] = 1;
0156 |     }
0157 |     const Eigen::Vector3d nq((*s.normals)[k].normal_x, (*s.normals)[k].normal_y, (*s.normals)[k].normal_z);
0158 |     if (dist_axis > 1e-12 && nq.allFinite() && nq.norm() > 1e-12)
0159 |       r.n_normal_ok += std::abs(nq.dot(radial) / (dist_axis * nq.norm())) >= cos_max ? 1 : 0;
0160 |   }
0161 |
0162 |   for (char b : arc) r.arc_deg += b ? 360.0 / kArcBins : 0.0;
0163 |   const double ratio = double(r.n_inliers) / double(r.n_points);
0164 |   // Post-fit filters not described in the paper (it only says "RANSAC cylinder
0165 |   // fitting"); on by default, each can be switched off.
0166 |   if (p.check_inlier_ratio && ratio < p.min_inlier_ratio) r.fail_mask |= 1u << int(FitFail::LowInlierRatio);
0167 |   if (p.check_radius && (r.radius < p.radius_min || r.radius > p.radius_max))
0168 |     r.fail_mask |= 1u << int(FitFail::RadiusOutOfRange);
0169 |   if (p.check_tilt && r.tilt_deg > p.max_tilt_deg) r.fail_mask |= 1u << int(FitFail::TiltTooLarge);
0170 |   // Our additions (not in the paper): metrics are always computed and written
0171 |   // to the diagnostics CSV; they only reject a fit when enabled.
0172 |   if (p.check_normal_consistency && double(r.n_normal_ok) / double(r.n_points) < p.min_normal_ratio)
0173 |     r.fail_mask |= 1u << int(FitFail::NormalInconsistent);
0174 |   if (p.check_arc_coverage && r.arc_deg < p.min_arc_deg) r.fail_mask |= 1u << int(FitFail::ArcCoverageLow);
0175 |   for (int f = 1; f < int(FitFail::Count); ++f)
0176 |     if (r.fail_mask & (1u << f)) {
0177 |       r.fail = FitFail(f);
0178 |       break;
0179 |     }
0180 |   return r;
0181 | }
0182 |
0183 | std::vector<std::vector<int>> drawSamplesForTest(const Cloud& cloud, const NormalCloud& normals, std::uint32_t seed,
0184 |                                                  int n) {

SHA256：40847accbcecec4a57b37074082df8ba85a95fb2801281d9d5321c9b9e908e80

0001 | #include "cm/tree_position.hpp"
0002 |
0003 | #include <cmath>
0004 |
0005 | namespace cm {
0006 |
0007 | std::optional<IntersectResult> axisDtmIntersection(const Eigen::Vector3d& p, const Eigen::Vector3d& v_in, const Dtm& dtm,
0008 |                                                    const IntersectParams& ip, double t_ref) {
0009 |   Eigen::Vector3d v = v_in.normalized();
0010 |   if (v.z() < 0) v = -v;
0011 |   if (v.z() < 1e-6) return std::nullopt;  // horizontal axis
0012 |   auto f = [&](double t) -> std::optional<double> {
0013 |     const Eigen::Vector3d x = p + t * v;
0014 |     const auto h = dtm.height(x.x(), x.y());
0015 |     if (!h) return std::nullopt;
0016 |     return x.z() - *h;
0017 |   };
0018 |   IntersectResult r;
0019 |   // 1. fixed point, starting from the DTM height below the reference point.
0020 |   double t = t_ref;
0021 |   for (int k = 0; k < ip.max_iter; ++k) {
0022 |     const Eigen::Vector3d x = p + t * v;
0023 |     const auto h = dtm.height(x.x(), x.y());
0024 |     if (!h) break;
0025 |     const double tn = (*h - p.z()) / v.z();
0026 |     r.iterations = k + 1;
0027 |     const auto fn = f(tn);
0028 |     if (fn && std::abs(*fn) <= ip.tol) {
0029 |       r.point = p + tn * v;
0030 |       return r;
0031 |     }
0032 |     if (!std::isfinite(tn) || std::abs(tn - t_ref) > ip.search_range) break;
0033 |     t = tn;
0034 |   }
0035 |   // 2. bracket scan + bisection.
0036 |   r.used_bisection = true;
0037 |   const int steps = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(ip.search_range / ip.scan_step));
0038 |   // Scan outward from t_ref so the bracket nearest the stem is found first.
0039 |   for (int k = 0; k < steps; ++k) {
0040 |     for (int sgn : {-1, 1}) {
0041 |       const double a = t_ref + sgn * k * ip.scan_step, b = t_ref + sgn * (k + 1) * ip.scan_step;
0042 |       const auto fa = f(a), fb = f(b);
0043 |       if (!fa || !fb || (*fa > 0) == (*fb > 0)) continue;
0044 |       double lo = a, hi = b, flo = *fa;
0045 |       for (int it = 0; it < 200; ++it) {
0046 |         const double mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi);
0047 |         const auto fm = f(mid);
0048 |         if (!fm) return std::nullopt;
0049 |         r.iterations++;
0050 |         if (std::abs(*fm) <= ip.tol || std::abs(hi - lo) < 1e-9) {
0051 |           r.point = p + mid * v;
0052 |           return r;
0053 |         }
0054 |         if ((*fm > 0) == (flo > 0)) { lo = mid; flo = *fm; }
0055 |         else hi = mid;
0056 |       }
0057 |     }
0058 |   }
0059 |   return std::nullopt;
0060 | }
0061 |
0062 | }  // namespace cm

0011 | struct Index {
0012 |   Cloud::Ptr cloud{new Cloud};pcl::search::KdTree<PointT> tree;
0013 |   explicit Index(const SurfaceLevel& level) {
0014 |     cloud->reserve(level.points.size());for(const auto& q:level.points)cloud->push_back(PointT(q.p.x(),q.p.y(),q.p.z()));

0018 | std::vector<std::size_t> sample(std::size_t n,std::size_t cap) {
0019 |   std::vector<std::size_t> ids;const auto k=std::min(n,cap);ids.reserve(k);
0020 |   for(std::size_t i=0;i<k;++i)ids.push_back(i*n/k);return ids;

0022 | GeometryScore directional(const SurfaceLevel& source,const SurfaceLevel& target,const RigidTransform& pose,const Index& index,double gate,std::size_t cap) {
0023 |   const auto ids=sample(source.points.size(),cap);std::size_t support=0;double loss=0;
0024 | #pragma omp parallel for reduction(+:support,loss) schedule(static)
0025 |   for(std::size_t k=0;k<ids.size();++k) {
0026 |     const auto& s=source.points[ids[k]];const auto p=pose.apply(s.p);pcl::Indices ix(1);std::vector<float> ds(1);
0027 |     if(index.tree.nearestKSearch(PointT(p.x(),p.y(),p.z()),1,ix,ds)==1 && ds[0]<gate*gate &&
0028 |        std::abs((pose.R*s.n).dot(target.points[ix[0]].n))>.7) {++support;loss+=ds[0];}
0029 |   }
0030 |   return {ids.empty()?0:double(support)/ids.size(),support?std::sqrt(loss/support):1e9,support};
0031 | }
0032 | }
0033 | GeometryScore geometryScore(const SurfaceLevel& source,const SurfaceLevel& target,const RigidTransform& pose) {
0034 |   if(source.points.empty()||target.points.empty())return {};
0035 |   // Horizontal ground is common even at wrong offsets in planted forests.
0036 |   // Verify vertical structure separately; the optimizer below still uses all
0037 |   // surface orientations and estimates all six pose degrees of freedom.
0038 |   SurfaceLevel vertical_source,vertical_target;vertical_source.voxel=source.voxel;vertical_target.voxel=target.voxel;
0039 |   for(const auto& p:source.points)if(std::abs(p.n.z())<.3)vertical_source.points.push_back(p);
0040 |   for(const auto& p:target.points)if(std::abs(p.n.z())<.3)vertical_target.points.push_back(p);
0041 |   const bool enough=vertical_source.points.size()>=30&&vertical_target.points.size()>=30;
0042 |   const auto& alevel=enough?vertical_source:source;const auto& blevel=enough?vertical_target:target;
0043 |   Index si(alevel),ti(blevel);RigidTransform inv;inv.R=pose.R.transpose();inv.t=-inv.R*pose.t;
0044 |   const double gate=std::min(source.voxel,.2);
0045 |   const auto a=directional(alevel,blevel,pose,ti,gate,8000),b=directional(blevel,alevel,inv,si,gate,8000);
0046 |   return {std::sqrt(a.overlap*b.overlap),(a.rmse+b.rmse)/2,std::min(a.support,b.support)};
0047 | }
0048 | RefineResult refineGeometry(const std::vector<SurfaceLevel>& source,const std::vector<SurfaceLevel>& target,const RigidTransform& initial) {
0049 |   RefineResult result;result.pose=initial;if(source.size()!=3||target.size()!=3)return result;
0050 |   result.before=geometryScore(source.back(),target.back(),initial);
0051 |   const int budget[3]={30,25,20};
0052 |   for(std::size_t level=0;level<3;++level) {
0053 |     const auto& src=source[level];const auto& tgt=target[level];if(src.points.size()<30||tgt.points.size()<30)continue;
0054 |     const auto ids=sample(src.points.size(),20000);Index index(tgt);const double gate=3*src.voxel;
0055 |     struct Match {Eigen::Vector3d p,q,n;double d=0,r=0;int target=-1;};
0056 |     for(int iteration=0;iteration<budget[level];++iteration) {
0057 |       std::vector<Match> candidates(ids.size());std::vector<char> valid(ids.size(),0);
0058 | #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
0059 |       for(std::size_t k=0;k<ids.size();++k) {
0060 |         const auto& original=src.points[ids[k]];const auto p=result.pose.apply(original.p);pcl::Indices ix(1);std::vector<float> ds(1);

+  const double gate=std::min(source.voxel,.2);
+  const auto a=directional(alevel,blevel,pose,ti,gate,8000),b=directional(blevel,alevel,inv,si,gate,8000);
   return {std::sqrt(a.overlap*b.overlap),(a.rmse+b.rmse)/2,std::min(a.support,b.support)};
 }
 RefineResult refineGeometry(const std::vector<SurfaceLevel>& source,const std::vector<SurfaceLevel>& target,const RigidTransform& initial) {
